---

Canadian computer science, software engineering and tech internships and co-ops for **Winter, Summer and Fall 2027**, plus **new grad and junior developer roles** — in Toronto, Waterloo, Ottawa, Montreal, Vancouver, Calgary, Edmonton, other Canadian cities, and remote roles open to Canadian applicants.

Covers backend, frontend and full-stack development, software engineering, data science, machine learning and AI, DevOps and cloud, QA, cybersecurity, mobile and IT — for Canadian and international students studying in Canada.

📅 **New to recruiting?** Read [when Winter, Summer and Fall 2027 postings open, term by term →](https://csjobs.ca/blog/tech-internships-canada-2027?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme)

> [!TIP]
> Applying to dozens of these? [csjobs.ca](https://csjobs.ca/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) scores your resume against **every role below**, shows the keywords you're missing, and tailors your resume to each posting — free to start.

### 📌 How this list works
- Pulled automatically from **40+ company career sites, ATS boards and job feeds**, checked **every hour** — no waiting for someone to add it.
- **Only open roles.** Closed postings are removed within the hour, so you never click through to a dead link.
- Sorted newest first; roles at the same company are grouped with ↳.
- 🔥 = posted in the last 48 hours · ✨ = big-name company (FAANG, unicorns, Fortune 500) — full details with [csjobs.ca Pro](https://csjobs.ca/upgrade?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme)

**Jump to:** [🎓 Internships & Co-ops (1018)](#-internships--co-ops) · [🚀 New Grad & Junior (273)](#-new-grad--junior-roles)

**By city:** [Toronto](https://csjobs.ca/internships/toronto?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) · [Waterloo](https://csjobs.ca/internships/waterloo?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) · [Ottawa](https://csjobs.ca/internships/ottawa?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) · [Montreal](https://csjobs.ca/internships/montreal?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) · [Vancouver](https://csjobs.ca/internships/vancouver?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) · [Calgary](https://csjobs.ca/internships/calgary?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) · [Remote](https://csjobs.ca/internships/remote?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme) · [International students](https://csjobs.ca/internships/international-students?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme)
